4:T707,The comparison includes four families: byte-level BPE as the baseline, parity-aware BPE as a script-rebalanced BPE variant, MYTE as a morphology-driven tokenizer, and BLT as a dynamic byte-patch representation that avoids a fixed vocabulary. The strength of the paper is the controlled setup.

Tokenizer training uses the same 1M-sentence, 3.5GB sample from mC4 across 11 Southeast Asian languages, sampled according to the original language proportions. Language-model training uses the same 100M-sentence, 203GB multilingual FineWeb2 sample, with temperature sampling (tau = 1.21) to upweight lower-resource languages. The authors report data size in sentences and bytes rather than tokens, which is the right choice because token count is itself the object of comparison.

Vocabulary size is matched where possible. MYTE is trained at 4,096 / 8,192 / 12,288 morphemes per language, corresponding to total inventories of roughly 45k / 90k / 135k. Byte-level BPE and parity-aware BPE are matched to those total sizes. BLT has no fixed vocabulary, so it is compared through average patch sizes of 4.5 / 6 / 8 bytes. For downstream LM training, the paper uses the 90k setting for the fixed-vocabulary tokenizers and the 4.5-byte setting for BLT.

The key methodological contribution is to connect intrinsic tokenizer analysis with downstream LM training. The paper does not stop at compression statistics; it trains 1.5B decoder-only models from scratch and compares what tokenization differences mean for actual multilingual modeling. It also reports preprocessing and tokenizer-training cost, which is important because some methods are much harder to deploy in practice: MYTE and BPE-style tokenizers can be trained on CPU, while BLT requires GPU-heavy entropy modeling and tokenization pipelines.5:T675,The study covers 11 Southeast Asian languages: English, Burmese, Chinese, Indonesian, Khmer, Lao, Malay, Tagalog, Tamil, Thai, and Vietnamese. It first evaluates intrinsic tokenizer properties, then trains 1.5B decoder-only language models for downstream comparison.

Several concrete numbers are available from the full text provided. Tokenizer training uses 1M sentences (3.5GB). LM training uses 100M sentences (203GB). The main downstream comparison uses the 90k tokenizer setting, close to the 100k–128k range common in modern LLMs. Training runs on 4–8 H100/H200 GPUs, with runtime normalized to an 8×H200 equivalent. MYTE is explicitly reported as the most expensive option at 300 normalized hours because it produces far more training tokens—269B tokens after tokenization. Byte-level BPE is the most efficient baseline. BLT also carries substantial systems overhead because its entropy tokenizer and large-scale patching pipeline require multiple H100/H200 GPUs.

The likely takeaway is not a single winner but a set of trade-offs. More equitable tokenizers improve token parity for underrepresented and non-Latin languages, but they differ in where the cost lands. Parity-aware BPE appears to be a lower-friction compromise. MYTE may better respect morphological structure, but at the cost of token explosion and slower training. BLT avoids fixed-vocabulary bias more fundamentally, but with higher systems complexity. The most informative results are therefore language-by-language compression disparity, total training token count, and whether downstream gains are consistent across languages rather than only on an aggregate average.6:T828,模型是 550B 总参数、每 token 激活 55B 参数的 Mixture-of-Experts hybrid Mamba-Attention LM。架构延续 Nemotron 3 Super 的设计，使用 LatentMoE 作为 MoE 层，以提升每个激活参数的有效容量；同时混合 Mamba 与 attention，目标是降低长序列推理中的 attention 计算和 KV cache 占用。模型还原生加入 Multi-Token Prediction。MTP 使用两个 head，且两个 head 共享参数，用于训练出更稳的自回归 draft token，服务于 speculative decoding。每个 MTP head 由一个 attention layer 和一个 MoE layer 组成。

预训练使用 20T text tokens，采用 Warmup-Stable-Decay 学习率计划。数据分两阶段：前 15T tokens 强调多样性和领域覆盖，后 5T tokens 强调高质量数据，用于提升模型精度。这种安排接近大规模训练中的“先覆盖、后打磨”：前段用广覆盖保证能力底座，后段用更干净、更高价值的数据降低噪声并改善 benchmark 表现。

低精度训练是报告的重点之一。Nemotron 3 Ultra 使用 NVFP4 预训练，NVFP4 层采用 E2M1 数据类型，对权重做二维 block quantization；wgrad 输入使用 Random Hadamard Transform；梯度使用 stochastic rounding。为了稳定性，最后 15% 网络，也就是 16 层，以及 Mamba output projection、latent projection、QKV 和 attention projection、MTP layers、embedding layers 保持更高精度。这个选择反映了一个工程判断：不是所有矩阵都适合压到 FP4，靠近输出端、注意力投影和嵌入相关层对数值误差更敏感。

后训练路径包括 curated SFT、统一 RLVR、多教师 on-policy distillation。RLVR 覆盖 reasoning、agentic、code、safety、usability、chat 等多类环境。团队还训练了十多个领域教师模型，包括沿专门 agentic SFT 路径训练的教师。MOPD 在学生自己生成的 rollout 上提供 dense token-level guidance，把多个教师的能力合并到 Ultra。模型还支持 reasoning effort control，用于在推理时调节准确率和计算量之间的取舍。7:T81d,Nemotron 3 Ultra is a 550B total-parameter MoE language model with 55B active parameters per token. It follows the Nemotron 3 Super hybrid Mamba-Attention design, scaled up and combined with LatentMoE. The motivation is direct: MoE improves accuracy per active parameter, while replacing part of full attention with Mamba-style sequence modeling reduces inference cost and KV-cache footprint for long contexts and long outputs.

The model includes native Multi-Token Prediction with two shared-parameter heads. The heads are trained to produce autoregressive draft tokens and are used for speculative decoding. Each MTP head consists of one attention layer followed by one MoE layer.

Pretraining uses 20 trillion text tokens with a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning-rate schedule. The first 15T tokens emphasize diversity and broad domain coverage. The last 5T tokens emphasize higher-quality data to refine accuracy. This is a practical data schedule: broad coverage first, lower-noise refinement later.

A central technical point is NVFP4 pretraining. NVFP4 layers use the E2M1 datatype with two-dimensional block quantization on weights. Random Hadamard Transforms are applied to inputs for weight-gradient computation, and gradients use stochastic rounding. The recipe keeps numerically sensitive parts in higher precision: the final 15% of the network, corresponding to 16 layers, plus Mamba output projections, latent projections, QKV and attention projections, MTP layers, and embeddings. This selective precision policy is likely as important as the FP4 datatype itself.

Post-training starts with curated SFT, then applies unified RLVR over reasoning, agentic, code, safety, usability, and chat environments. More than ten specialized teacher models are trained, including agentic teachers from a dedicated agentic SFT path. Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation then gives dense token-level supervision on rollouts generated by the student model. The final model also supports reasoning effort control, allowing inference-time adjustment of the compute-accuracy trade-off.8:T4e7,The main scale numbers are 550B total parameters, 55B active parameters, 20T pretraining tokens, and 1M-token context extension. The paper reports 5.9×, 4.8×, and 1.6× higher inference throughput than GLM-5.1-754B-A40B, Kimi-K2.6-1T-A32B, and Qwen-3.5-397B-17B respectively under an 8K input / 64K output setting, while maintaining broadly comparable accuracy.

For NVFP4 training health, the team branches from checkpoints at 5T, 10T, and 16T tokens, switches tensors to BF16, and continues training for 74B tokens. The relative training-loss gap between the NVFP4 run and the BF16 continuation averages below 0.4%. This is the key evidence that the FP4 recipe tracks a high-precision training trajectory closely enough at this scale.

The base model is reported to outperform several public base models, including DeepSeek v3.2, Mistral Large 3, Kimi-K2, and GLM-4.5, though the provided text does not include the full benchmark tables. One concrete data ablation is given: adding the Nemotron-Pretraining-Legal-v1 synthetic legal datasets to Nemotron 3 Nano improves a proxy LegalBench average from 64.6 to 74.7. The release also includes 173B tokens of fresh GitHub code data, specialized synthetic pretraining datasets, and post-training datasets.9:T62e,Given a sequence x, the method partitions tokens into a conditioning set x_c and an evaluation set x_e. The loss is applied only to tokens in x_e, since tokens in x_c are observed. ac-gpt creates a copy of the conditioning tokens, \bar{x}_c, and places these copies at the beginning of the attention order. All later tokens can attend to them. The copied tokens keep their original positional encodings, so the model knows where the observed tokens belong in the original sequence. The copied conditioning tokens use unrestricted bidirectional attention among themselves because they are all observed.

The evaluation tokens still follow the original left-to-right causal factorization. This is the main distinction from masked or denoising LMs: ac-gpt does not independently predict masked tokens and does not rely on a pseudo-likelihood approximation. It models the joint distribution over the evaluation set through an autoregressive chain, conditioned on an arbitrary observed subset.

The copy mechanism is not a cosmetic detail. If the model simply allowed every token to attend to the original conditioning tokens, multi-layer attention could leak information between evaluation tokens. For example, a conditioning token at position 3 may need to attend to positions 1 and 2 in order to predict position 4; in the next layer, position 1 could then indirectly receive information about position 2 through position 3. This violates the intended left-to-right factorization over x_e. Separating copied observed tokens from the original autoregressive stream avoids this leakage.a:T6e4,The method has two main components. The first is Reflective Masking (RM). At each denoising step and for each position, the model chooses among three actions: keep the current token, re-mask the current token for future resampling, or reveal a token if the position is currently masked. The key idea is that remasking is no longer a fixed heuristic based only on low confidence in still-masked positions; already denoised tokens can be actively reconsidered. This makes the denoising process partially reversible and turns generation into iterative local correction.

The second component is History Reference (HR), a parameter-free mechanism that aggregates intermediate decoding states into a history-aware representation. Instead of conditioning only on the current partially denoised sequence, the model also gets access to what each position looked like in previous steps and how stable those predictions were. This gives MDMs a temporal memory channel that AR models get implicitly through sequential context accumulation.

The training recipe is the most important part of the paper. The authors argue that inference-time rules alone are insufficient because existing MDMs were never trained for self-initiated multi-turn revision. They therefore introduce lightweight post-training centered on reflective masking, together with a data-generation pipeline designed to stay close to the model’s native output distribution. This is a good design choice: if training examples are built from artificial off-distribution corruptions, the model may only learn to repair synthetic noise rather than revise its own realistic mistakes. The paper instead tries to teach the model to detect and correct unreliable predictions arising from its own denoising dynamics.b:T47c,The evaluation spans image editing, Sudoku, and text generation, which is a sensible way to test whether the method is tied to one modality or one supervision regime. The main result is consistent: RM improves over baseline MDM decoding across all three settings, and HR is especially helpful when the task requires autonomous exploration rather than explicit guidance. In image editing, where instructions specify both what and where to modify, RM mainly improves localized edits and reduces unnecessary changes elsewhere. In Sudoku, the benefit is clearer as iterative conflict detection and local correction. In text generation, where supervision is weakest and the final answer is not directly hinted, RM better exposes the intended reasoning behavior: progressively refining a partially wrong state rather than restarting from scratch. The provided excerpt does not include enough complete tables to quote reliable numeric gains, so the safe takeaway is qualitative but still important: the paper demonstrates a distinct form of test-time scaling for MDMs, where extra compute is spent on selective revision instead of forward token expansion.c:T431,This one is worth a careful read. Start with the introduction to understand the paper’s framing: the advantage of MDMs is not merely parallel generation speed, but explicit revisability of intermediate states. Then go straight to the Reflective Masking section and read three pieces closely: the per-position keep/re-mask/reveal decision rule, the construction of History Reference, and the post-training objective plus data pipeline. Those sections determine whether the method is just a decoding trick or a transferable capability-enabling recipe. In the experiments, do not only look at final metrics; check whether the mechanism is consistent across tasks. Sudoku and text generation are especially informative because they expose self-correction more directly than image editing. The deeper questions to keep in mind are whether reflective remasking scales to larger language models, whether HR can be combined with external memory or verifiers, and whether this capability should eventually be moved earlier into pretraining rather than added only in post-training.d:T42e,The study is structured around three axes.
1) Scaling: fit scaling laws across model sizes and training budgets using two continuous targets—validation loss (short-context quality) and log(LongPPL) as a smoother proxy for long-context capability—avoiding noisy, discrete downstream scores.
2) Mechanistic analysis: use receptive-field constraints and layer-wise probing to localize where long-range information flows; trace retrieval heads over training to measure when long-range retrieval behavior forms.
3) Design implications: propose “Large-Window Laziness”—large SWA windows make local context sufficient for next-token prediction, weakening gradients that would otherwise push full-attention layers to learn long-range retrieval, delaying retrieval-head formation. Since full attention is shared across hybrids, final long-context performance converges given enough training. As a concrete design tweak, apply NoPE specifically to full-attention layers in a small-window SWA hybrid to improve long-context generalization with minimal short-context cost.e:T9b3,方法由两部分组成。第一部分是 typed latent operator。作者定义了三类操作：Og 对应 global orientation，用来在较早阶段建立整体解题方向；Os 对应 state update，用来更新当前推理状态；Op 对应 procedural abstraction，用来调用更偏程序化、模式化的求解过程。每个 operator 都不是输出离散 token，而是根据当前 decoding context 生成一段 operator-conditioned latent tokens，再把这些连续向量拼接回上下文，继续后续可见 token 的自回归生成。这里的关键不是“插入 latent token”本身，而是给 latent computation 加上功能标签，使不同 operator 学到不同的计算角色。

第二部分是 operator invocation policy。模型在每个解码步不再只在词表上做 next-token 预测，而是在“生成可见 token”与“调用某个 latent operator 并指定预算”之间做选择。也就是说，动作空间包含 visible decoding 和若干 latent actions，latent action 还带 budget 维度，决定插入多少 latent tokens。这样模型可以在简单位置直接输出文本，在困难位置先做内部计算，再回到显式生成。

训练分两阶段。Stage 1 先冻结主 LLM，只训练 latent operators 和 latent synthesis module，让每个 operator 对齐到预设的功能角色。这里作者引入 Latent Synthesis Model，把 operator identity 与当前状态映射成一段可被主模型消费的 latent token 序列。先冻结 backbone 的目的很明确：避免在早期把“operator 学什么”和“主模型如何适配 latent interface”纠缠在一起，降低训练不稳定性。Stage 2 再固定 Stage 1 学到的 latent interface，只训练调用策略，使用 GRPO 做策略优化。奖励来自最终任务表现，因此 policy 学的是何时调用、调用哪种 operator、预算给多少。由于 operator invocation 是稀疏事件，作者还加了 operator-anchored auxiliary objective 来稳定训练，避免 policy 在少量触发位置上梯度过弱或塌缩到单一动作。

和已有 latent reasoning 方法相比，区别主要有三点。其一，不是 fixed-position latent insertion，而是 online decision。其二，不是单一 latent mode，而是 typed operators。其三，不是只决定“要不要想”，而是连“想什么”和“想多久”一起学。这使它更像一个可调度的内部计算系统，而不是一个附加的 soft-thought 通道。f:T821,Tyler has two core components.

First, it introduces typed latent operators. The model can invoke three operator types: Og for global orientation, Os for state update, and Op for procedural abstraction. Each operator takes the current decoding state and produces a sequence of operator-conditioned latent tokens, which are appended to the context and consumed by the same autoregressive backbone. The key idea is not latent-token insertion by itself, but functional specialization: latent computation is decomposed into reusable roles rather than collapsed into a single generic latent mode.

Second, Tyler learns an operator-invocation policy. At each decoding step, the model chooses between emitting a visible token and invoking one of the latent operators with a certain budget. The budget controls how much latent computation is inserted, typically via the number of latent tokens or latent steps. This turns decoding into a mixed action process: direct visible generation for easy steps, internal computation for harder ones.

Training is split into two stages. In Stage 1, the backbone LM is frozen while the latent operators and the latent synthesis module are trained. This isolates the problem of learning a usable latent interface from the problem of policy learning. The latent synthesis module maps the current state plus operator identity into latent-token sequences that the frozen LM can consume. In Stage 2, the learned latent interface is fixed and only the invocation policy is optimized with GRPO. The policy learns when to call an operator, which operator to call, and how much budget to allocate, using task-level reward. Because operator calls are sparse events, the paper adds an operator-anchored auxiliary objective to stabilize learning and prevent collapse or weak gradients at invocation positions.

Relative to prior work, the differences are clear: online invocation instead of fixed insertion points, typed operators instead of a single latent mode, and joint learning of whether, what, and how much to compute instead of a simple trigger mechanism.10:T6f2,The experimental story centers on three claims: gains across multiple backbones and reasoning benchmarks, transfer beyond the training domain, and better sequential adaptation. From the provided full-text excerpt, the paper reports consistent improvements over strong baselines across several model families and tasks.

The most interesting analysis is behavioral specialization of the learned policy. Operator usage varies with task difficulty and decoding stage. Easier steps rely more on direct visible decoding, while harder steps trigger more latent computation. The three operators are used differently: Og appears earlier as a planning or orientation mechanism, Os is used more for intermediate state refinement, and Op is associated with more procedural or template-like reasoning segments. This supports the claim that the typed operators acquire differentiated functional roles rather than acting as arbitrary labels.

The second notable result is in sequential adaptation across domains such as code, science, math, and theorem reasoning. Tyler reaches higher final performance with lower forgetting than baselines. That is especially relevant for foundation-model researchers, because it suggests that typed latent computation may provide a more reusable internal interface under domain shift.

The excerpt does not expose the full numeric tables, so it would be unsafe to invent exact gains, model sizes, or dataset scales. When reading the paper, the main things to verify are: which backbones were used, how training and transfer domains were separated, how much Tyler improves over fixed latent and untyped latent baselines, and whether the budget ablations show that the benefit comes from adaptive allocation rather than simply spending more test-time compute.11:T5ff,This is a skim paper, but it deserves a careful skim. A good pass takes about 35 to 50 minutes. Start with the introduction and the main framework figure to pin down the paper’s actual claim. The important shift is not “latent tokens exist,” but “latent computation is typed and scheduled online.”

In the method section, focus on three pieces. First, how the three operators are parameterized and how the latent synthesis module turns operator identity plus current state into latent-token sequences. Second, the action space of the invocation policy: whether budget is discrete or continuous, how policy decisions are integrated with token decoding, and what exactly GRPO is optimizing. Third, the operator-anchored auxiliary loss, since sparse invocation is where many RL-style control schemes become unstable.

In the experiments, prioritize the main comparison table, operator ablations, budget ablations, transfer results, and sequential adaptation. If time is limited, skip benchmark boilerplate and read only enough to answer three questions: does typed latent reasoning beat untyped latent reasoning, does adaptive invocation beat fixed schedules, and does the continual-adaptation gain come from reduced forgetting rather than a stronger starting point.

The two deeper follow-up questions are whether these operators really become transferable computational primitives, and whether the same framework could be moved earlier into pretraining or mid-training instead of being used only at reasoning fine-tuning time.12:T553,Core method is Expert Tying: share gate/up/down projection weights of all MoE expert FFNs across a group of g consecutive Transformer layers via Python-level pointer aliasing. Only optimizer logic needs adjustment to correctly accumulate gradients from tied parameters across multiple use sites; no other training or inference infrastructure changes are required. Per-layer routers, attention projections, and RMSNorm parameters remain independent, so hidden states still pass through g distinct layer operators. Controlled component ablations confirm the feasibility of this design: per-layer attention (not routing) is the primary source of layer distinctiveness, so sharing the large expert FFN parameter pool incurs minimal quality cost, while sharing attention causes order-of-magnitude larger degradation. Two key design rules are validated: 1) leave the first 2 and last 2 layers untied (2+2 prelude/coda), which delivers the single largest architectural performance gain; 2) heterogeneous width expansion: reinvest parameters saved by tying into more experts in middle tied layers, which consistently outperforms untied baselines at iso-parameter count. Unlike prior MoE cross-layer sharing work that either ties full layer blocks or a single fixed component, this work provides controlled, cross-architecture, multi-granularity analysis of sharing costs.13:T45d,Proposes ASRD (Anchor Supervised Revocable Decoding), a fully embedding-space framework compatible with FlashAttention, requiring no model retraining and working as a drop-in module for existing dLLMs. The core component is the dynamic Anchor Tokens Cache (ATC): tokens that maintain temporal consistency (stable predictions across multiple decoding steps) are promoted to trusted anchors and stored in the cache to form a reliable context skeleton. Two complementary embedding-level strategies are built on the ATC: (1) Anchor-Guided Generation injects entropy-weighted anchor signals into [MASK] token embeddings to reduce interference from erroneous tentative tokens, mitigating Error Propagation; (2) Anchor-Perturbed Verification probes tentative tokens with an orthogonal anchor-derived perturbation, only revoking tokens that rely on local error clusters rather than anchor-supported global consistency, addressing Local Error Reinforcement. The core difference from prior work is that ASRD first isolates trusted anchor context, instead of applying uniform generation and verification logic across all tokens.14:T565,The judge is queried on a content pair (u,v) under both presentation orders o∈{(u,v),(v,u)} with outputs in {1,2,tie}. A key methodological point is to map slot-level decisions back to canonical content identity before computing stability metrics.
1) Judge Datasheet protocol:
- A0 true-vacuum: empty/whitespace/identical-answer pairs to measure dark current (non-tie rate when tie is allowed).
- A1 controlled quality ladder: a constructively designed stimulus ladder (prefix-chain checklist with Pareto dominance, Δ0 controls, fillers, validity gates) to measure target sensitivity across increasing ΔQ.
- Criterion-shift probing: vary tie instructions (e.g., strict tie) to observe how the operating point moves—often reducing Δ0 false preferences while absorbing marginal ΔQ signals into ties.
2) Direction-stability decomposition (core technical novelty): raw same-quality non-tie rate (raw Δ0 FP) is decomposed into interpretable components:
- Stable cross-sensitivity (content-stable preference under order reversal).
- Positional false preference (slot-stable choice under order reversal → position bias).
- One-sided commit (only one order yields a non-tie → instability/near-threshold behavior).
- Residual conflict/other terms.
This prevents mislabeling “raw Δ0 non-ties” as cross-sensitivity when they may be dominated by position bias or instability.15:T6a4,The core artifact is the GRACE benchmark, together with a data-driven error taxonomy. The benchmark covers chains of thought from ten models over four source datasets spanning two reasoning settings: evidence-grounded reasoning and deductive reasoning. Each step in a trace is annotated with three pieces of information: a faithfulness label, an error category, and a natural-language explanation.

The taxonomy is discovered bottom-up rather than imposed manually. The authors first collect free-form critiques of many unfaithful steps using a strong LLM judge. These critiques are then embedded and clustered, yielding two empirically distinct error tracks: GRACE-Inference and GRACE-Grounding. The former captures deductive failures, logical leaps, and constraint violations. The latter captures factual contradiction, fabricated evidence, and extraction errors. This is a sensible design choice because it lets the taxonomy emerge from observed failure patterns rather than from a predefined label set.

For data construction, the paper uses multiple LLM judges to scale annotation. Cases with unanimous judge agreement form a large training set. Hard disagreement cases are sent to human annotators and become the high-quality evaluation set. This creates a practical split: scalable supervision for training, and more reliable, more difficult data for evaluation. The benchmark is then used in two ways. First, the authors train step-level evaluators and show that smaller models fine-tuned on the training set can approach larger ones. Second, they inject context-faithfulness signals into RL for downstream reasoning tasks and study the effect on both task accuracy and reasoning reliability.16:T698,The most important findings are qualitative and structural rather than a single headline number. First, the GRACE evaluation set reveals clear headroom for current models. A nontrivial fraction of traces that end with the correct answer still contain unfaithful intermediate steps, while some fully faithful traces still end in the wrong answer. This shows that answer accuracy is not a sufficient proxy for reasoning faithfulness.

Second, the taxonomy appears empirically meaningful. By clustering critiques of unfaithful steps, the authors find that inference-related failures and grounding-related failures separate into distinct groups rather than collapsing into one generic error label. That makes the benchmark useful not only for binary detection but also for diagnosis.

Third, there are two training-related results. Fine-tuning smaller evaluator models on the GRACE training set can bring them close to larger models, suggesting that step-level faithfulness judgment is learnable and can be distilled. In addition, incorporating step-level context-faithfulness signals into RL improves both downstream task accuracy and reasoning reliability. The introduction does not expose all the detailed numbers, so the main tables in the full paper are worth checking. The key point is not just the absolute gain, but that the reward appears to improve reliability without simply pushing the model toward shorter or overly cautious responses.

One caveat is that the paper is centered on benchmark construction and evaluator training, not on a new reasoning architecture. For a foundation-model reader, the main value lies in how it defines a trainable process-level supervision signal.17:T52e,ReGrad has an offline and an online phase. Offline, the system computes document-specific gradients and stores them in an indexed Gradient Bank. At inference time, a query retrieves relevant gradients. These gradients are temporarily applied to the model weights, the model generates an answer, and the adapted weights are discarded afterward. This avoids accumulating unrelated document updates into a single global parameter state.

A key point is that the method does not rely on raw next-token language-modeling gradients alone. Such gradients are optimized for reconstructing documents and may overfit surface token patterns. Downstream use requires question answering, factual extraction, and reasoning over document content. ReGrad therefore introduces a bi-level meta-learning objective: the inner step applies a document-induced gradient, while the outer objective evaluates the adapted model on downstream supervision. This trains the gradient construction and application process so that temporary adaptation improves task behavior rather than only document likelihood.

The method can be used without retrieved text, as a parameter-only adaptation mechanism. It can also be combined with in-context RAG, where retrieved documents provide textual evidence and retrieved gradients provide parameter-space adaptation.18:T407,BALTO extracts checkable factual claims from generated responses, verifies them against provided evidence, and maps the verification results back to token-level signals. Its main mechanism is balanced token-level policy optimization. Hallucinated tokens receive negative advantages, faithful tokens receive positive compensatory advantages, and neutral tokens are excluded from the update. The total positive and negative faithfulness-driven signals are balanced within each response.

This makes the update act like a redistribution of probability mass: lower the probability of unsupported spans and raise the probability of supported spans, instead of suppressing the entire answer. The design directly addresses two weaknesses of response-level RL. First, a local factual error can contaminate gradients for all tokens. Second, a model can reduce penalties by becoming shorter or less informative. BALTO uses token localization to reduce irrelevant updates and balanced advantages to control length and faithful-density biases.19:T8ba,方法核心是用 Boundless Distributed Alignment Search 去找三类候选机制在 residual stream 中的因果子空间。作者把先前行为研究里的三种解释，重写成 mechanistic hypotheses：G 表示 group entity binding，即模型是否把“实体+其 pronoun”作为一个绑定单元来检索；R 表示 recency bias，即是否更容易复制最近出现的 pronoun；S 表示 stereotypical bias，即是否受职业-性别统计关联驱动。

DAS 的基本思想是：在某一层 residual representation 上学习一个正交旋转，把高层概念对应的信息压到某个可识别子空间里；然后做 interchange intervention，把 source 输入中的该子空间替换进 base 输入，观察输出是否按预期改变。如果替换后输出跟高层因果假设一致，就说明这个子空间在因果上承载了该机制。Boundless DAS 进一步把子空间维度 k 也做成可学习的软边界，而不是手工枚举。

这篇工作的价值在于它不是只找“有没有一个 pronoun neuron/head”，而是把机制视为 distributed subspace，并且允许多个机制同时存在。作者发现三种机制都能在不同深度找到因果子空间，没有任何一种单独解释全部行为。进一步，他们把这些机制组合成 mixture model，可以解释绝大多数模型行为。

除了 DAS，作者还做了 attention head 分析，发现 pronoun 信息到达答案位置主要有两条竞争路径：一条复制 occupation-pronoun pair 这样的语义绑定单元，另一条更像表面形式复制，直接沿最近或显著的 pronoun token 传播。这个分析把“为什么模型会在某些例子里忠实、在另一些例子里失真”讲得更细：不是单一模块失效，而是多条回路竞争，哪条回路占优取决于上下文结构和模型偏置。

实验对象覆盖十个 instruction-tuned decoder-only 模型，来自 Llama-3.1、Gemma-2、Qwen2.5、OLMo-2 四个家族，规模从 1B 到 72B。评测时不是直接看自由生成，而是比较 pronoun 选项的 log-prob，避免无关 token 抢占概率质量。这个设计更适合做机制分析，因为它把输出空间限制在真正关心的 pronoun 决策上。1a:T8a3,The main tool is Boundless Distributed Alignment Search (DAS). The authors formalize three candidate mechanisms as mechanistic hypotheses: G for group entity binding, where the model retrieves an entity together with its associated pronoun; R for recency bias, where the model prefers the most recently seen pronoun; and S for stereotypical bias, where occupation-linked gender associations influence the prediction.

DAS works by learning an orthogonal rotation of the residual stream at a chosen layer so that a high-level concept becomes localized in a subspace. The method then performs interchange interventions: it takes a base input, swaps in the learned subspace from a source input, rotates back, and checks whether the output changes in the way predicted by the causal hypothesis. If it does, that subspace is treated as causally carrying the mechanism. Boundless DAS extends this by learning the effective subspace size with differentiable soft boundaries instead of manually searching over dimensions.

A strong point of the paper is that it does not look for a single neuron or a single head. It treats mechanisms as distributed subspaces and allows multiple mechanisms to coexist. The results support exactly that picture: all three mechanisms are present, distributed across depth, and none alone explains the full behavior.

The paper also adds an attention-head analysis. It finds two competing routes by which pronoun information reaches the answer position. One route copies an occupation-pronoun pair as a semantic unit, which is closer to correct referent tracking. The other route copies the surface form more directly, which makes the model vulnerable to recency effects and stereotypical interference. This gives a more concrete circuit-level story for why the same model can be faithful in some contexts and fail badly in others.

The experiments cover ten instruction-tuned decoder-only models from four families: Llama-3.1, Gemma-2, Qwen2.5, and OLMo-2, ranging from 1B to 72B. Outputs are scored by comparing log-probabilities over pronoun options rather than unrestricted generation, which is a sensible choice for mechanism analysis because it isolates the pronoun decision itself.1b:T69e,There are three main findings. First, all three mechanisms—group entity binding, recency bias, and stereotypical bias—can be recovered as causal subspaces with Boundless DAS, and these subspaces are distributed across model depth rather than localized to a single layer. Second, no single mechanism explains model behavior on its own, but a mixture model combining them predicts 91% to 99.5% of behavior. That is the central empirical result: pronoun fidelity is not governed by one dominant heuristic, but by multiple simultaneously active mechanisms. Third, the attention analysis identifies two competing copying pathways, one semantic and one surface-form based, which helps explain when models stay faithful and when they drift.

The study builds diagnostic counterfactual datasets from RUFF, a pronoun-fidelity dataset with more than 5 million instances. Earlier behavioral work had already shown that adding a single distractor entity with a different pronoun can reduce accuracy sharply, with a cited drop of up to 34%. This paper uses that structured setup not to improve task accuracy, but to train and validate causal interventions.

For a foundation-model reader, the most useful pattern is not which model family scores highest. It is that the same three mechanisms appear across families and scales, suggesting that these are not isolated quirks of one architecture. Another useful point is that the mechanisms are distributed, which implies that patching one head or one layer is unlikely to fully fix the problem. The appendix is likely where the most informative details live: layerwise IIA, learned subspace sizes, and how the mixture weights differ across model families.1c:T51b,This is a skim paper and can be read in about 30 to 45 minutes. Start with the task setup. The important point is that pronoun fidelity is a deliberately minimal setting designed to separate entity binding, recency copying, and stereotype effects, not just another coreference benchmark.

In the method section, focus on DAS and Boundless DAS. Three things matter: how the high-level causal hypotheses are converted into base/source/counterfactual examples, how the rotation and soft boundaries are learned, and how to interpret Interchange Intervention Accuracy. Once those are clear, the rest of the paper becomes much easier to follow.

In the experiments, prioritize the layerwise IIA results for the three mechanisms, the mixture-model explanation rate, and the attention-head evidence for the two copying routes. If time is short, you can skip many of the per-model details and just verify the main claim that multiple separable mechanisms coexist and jointly explain behavior.

The two follow-up questions worth carrying into pretraining research are: why standard next-token training learns both entity-binding behavior and shallow surface-copy shortcuts at the same time, and whether data design, objective changes, or mid-training interventions could shift that balance toward more robust binding.1d:T59d,The setup is intentionally minimal. The authors compare three existing online augmentation methods—AWM, ASI, and ReasoningBank—against a budget-matched vanilla baseline called Vanilla-IB. The augmented agents keep the default actor horizon of up to 10 steps, while spending additional tokens on workflow induction, skill extraction, memory retrieval, reasoning-bank updates, and related control logic. Vanilla-IB removes all auxiliary modules and reallocates the same overall budget to a longer actor interaction horizon.

That design choice is the core contribution. The paper does not propose a new agent architecture; it proposes a stricter control condition. Instead of comparing “augmented agent vs plain actor with a smaller budget,” it compares “augmented agent vs plain actor with the same total token budget.” The study is explicitly online: tasks are processed sequentially, and any reusable knowledge must be accumulated from earlier evaluation trajectories rather than built offline in advance. This makes the trade-off sharper, because online augmentation is sensitive to task order and cannot amortize its cost over a precompiled skill library.

The paper’s methodological contribution is therefore an evaluation protocol with three requirements: report total token usage across all modules, compare methods under matched budgets, and report multi-run variance rather than relying on a single aggregate score.1e:T572,The main benchmark is WebArena, evaluated on three domains: Shopping (187 tasks), Reddit (106 tasks), and Admin (182 tasks). The study uses three base models: Gemini 3 Flash, GPT-5.4-mini, and Qwen 3.6-27B. Across these settings, the headline result is consistent: Vanilla-IB achieves the best aggregate success rate overall while often using fewer total tokens than AWM, ASI, and ReasoningBank. In other words, once auxiliary token overhead is counted, spending the budget on more direct interaction is usually better than spending it on online memory or skill machinery.

The authors also test the same question on WorkArena-L1, a 33-task ServiceNow benchmark covering enterprise workflows such as dashboards, forms, knowledge-base search, filtering, sorting, menu navigation, and service-catalog requests. With Qwen 3.6-27B, Vanilla-IB remains competitive with the best augmented methods, suggesting the budget-matching issue is not specific to consumer-style websites.

A second important result concerns variance. The paper argues that single-run success rates hide substantial task-level variability, especially in online settings where later performance depends on earlier trajectories. While the provided text does not include the full numeric tables, the qualitative conclusion is clear: single-run aggregate comparisons are too weak to support strong claims about online augmentation.1f:T7ad,方法上有三块。第一块是 ForgetEval。作者把 forgetting 拆成 5 个结构家族：supersession、decay、amnesia、purge、drift；再加一层 10 类 adversarial attack，例如 substring trap、prefix collision、paraphrase supersession、negation trap、temporal qualifier、identifier obfuscation、cross-lingual 等。每个 case 都是确定性的 dataclass：先写入 setup facts 和 distractors，再执行 mutation，再看 final query 的 top-10 recall 是否满足 must_contain / must_not_contain。整个评分不用 LLM judge，靠确定性字符串匹配完成，这一点很重要，因为它把“评测对象”和“评测器”解耦了。

第二块是 Adapter Protocol。作者定义了一个六方法接口，把不同 memory backend 统一到同一行为契约下。即便某个系统没有原生 supersede，也可以用 add+delete 组合实现并接受同样测试；没有某个 primitive 的系统则记为 N/A，而不是硬算失败。这个设计避免了把“接口缺失”和“实现错误”混在一起。

第三块是 13 种系统配置的架构比较。作者把它们归成几类：无删除、deterministic、vec-only、inscribe-time LLM、KG abstraction、joint、mutation-time LLM hook。论文的关键观察是三种 placement regime 呈现互补覆盖，而不是简单强弱排序。deterministic primitive 对 lexical/temporal 类问题足够，但在 canonicalization 上几乎失效；inscribe-time LLM 能在写入时把别名、改写、跨表述归一化，因此 canonicalization 类能做到 100%，但它无法理解后续删除意图；mutation-time hook 则在删除时调用 LLM，把自然语言删除命令映射到精确 mutation，因此能处理 intent-aware deletion，同时保持 recall hot path 不变。两种不同 backend 只要共享这个窄 JSON mutation hook，整体表现会收敛到接近的水平，这说明关键不是底层存储形态，而是控制面接口设计。20:T7fb,The work has three main components. The first is ForgetEval. Forgetting is decomposed into five structural families: supersession, decay, amnesia, purge, and drift. On top of that, the benchmark adds ten adversarial attack categories such as substring traps, prefix collisions, paraphrase supersession, negation traps, temporal qualifiers, identifier obfuscation, and cross-lingual variants. Each case is a deterministic dataclass with setup facts, distractors, mutations, a final query, and must-contain / must-not-contain constraints. A case passes if the top-10 recall blob satisfies those constraints. Importantly, scoring is deterministic string matching rather than LLM judging.

The second component is the Adapter Protocol. It defines a six-method behavioral interface that allows heterogeneous memory backends to be compared under the same contract. Systems without a native supersede primitive can still implement it compositionally via add plus delete. Missing primitives are marked as N/A rather than counted as failures. This avoids conflating interface absence with implementation failure.

The third component is the architectural comparison across thirteen system configurations. These are grouped into regimes such as no-deletion, deterministic, vec-only, inscribe-time LLM, KG abstraction, joint, and mutation-time LLM hook. The key finding is that these regimes provide complementary coverage rather than a simple ranking. Deterministic primitives handle lexical and temporal cases well but fail on canonicalization. Inscribe-time LLMs normalize aliases and paraphrases at write time, so they recover canonicalization failures, but they cannot interpret later deletion intent. Mutation-time LLM hooks instead parse deletion intent at update time and map it to precise mutations while leaving the recall hot path unchanged. Two different backends converge to similar performance once they share the same narrow JSON-shaped mutation hook, suggesting that control-plane interface design matters more than the storage substrate.21:T6c0,Four experimental results stand out. First, the benchmark itself contains 1,385 English cases: 1,000 templated cases and 385 adversarial ones. The adversarial layer includes 132 hand-crafted core cases and 253 LLM-drafted but oracle-validated cases. Qwen-2.5-72B is used only for data admission, not for evaluation. Human agreement is strong, with Fleiss' kappa of 0.958 on a 100-case, 10-annotator audit.

Second, the paper shows a clear decoupling between recall quality and forgetting ability. MemPalace scores 60/150 on Memora, indicating strong recall, yet gets 0/385 on adversarial forgetting. That is the cleanest evidence in the paper that retrieval success says little about controllable forgetting.

Third, the main comparison across thirteen configurations is informative at the category level. Deterministic primitives work for lexical and temporal categories, but identifier obfuscation stays at or below 5%, and cross-lingual cases are at 0%. Inscribe-time LLMs recover canonicalization to 100% but do not help with intent-sensitive mutation. Mutation-time LLM hooks reach 93.3% to 94.2% overall when excluding the primitive-existence compound_fact category, and 91.7% to 93.2% when including it. The reported cost is about $0.17 per 385-case run, with the recall hot path unchanged.

Fourth, the category-wise analysis is more important than the average score. Canonicalization depends on semantic normalization at write time. Intent-aware deletion depends on semantic interpretation at mutation time. Lexical and temporal correctness can often be handled by deterministic rules. The paper's practical lesson is therefore about where to place intelligence, not about replacing the entire memory stack with an LLM 中一个长期被低估但很基础的问题：tokenizer 的结构偏置会系统性抬高非拉丁、低资源语言的 token 长度，从而同时影响训练效率、推理成本、有效上下文长度和跨语言能力公平性。过去不少工作各自提出 parity-aware BPE、形态学驱动分词、BLT 这类替代方案，但通常只和 byte-level BPE 做单边比较，缺少在统一数据、统一词表规模、统一训练预算下的直接对照。作者要回答的不是“某个新 tokenizer 能否赢过 BPE”，而是“在真实多语言建模约束下，不同公平化 tokenizer 到底带来什么代价和收益，收益出现在哪一层：压缩率、公平性、还是下游建模表现”。","importance":"对预训练研究，这篇工作的价值是明确且直接的。第一，它把 tokenizer 从工程前处理重新拉回到 pretrain recipe 的核心变量：不同分词方案不仅改变 token 数，还改变每种语言在固定 compute 下实际得到的训练信号密度。第二，它把“公平性”具体化为可测的 token compression disparity，而不是停留在抽象伦理表述；这对 multilingual scaling、data mixture、context allocation 都有实际意义。第三，它提醒一个常被忽略的取舍：更公平的分词未必带来更高的系统效率，因为 token 数、训练时长、硬件需求和实现复杂度可能显著上升。对做多语言基座的人，这篇论文更像一份 controlled study：它不提供终局答案，但把 tokenizer 选择中的几条主要 trade-off 摆到了同一张桌面上。","technical_approach":"作者比较了四类方案：Byte-level BPE 作为基线，Parity-aware BPE 作为对脚本频率偏置做重平衡的 BPE 变体，MYTE 作为形态学驱动的分词方法，以及 BLT 这种不依赖固定词表、改用动态 byte patch 的表示方式。设计上最重要的是控制变量。\n\nTokenizer 训练数据统一来自 11 种东南亚语言的 mC4 子集，共 100 万句、3.5GB，按 mC4 原始语言比例采样。语言模型训练数据统一来自 FineWeb2，共 1 亿句、203GB，并用 temperature sampling（τ=1.21）提升低资源语言占比。作者特意按句子数和字节数而不是 token 数报告数据规模，这是合理的，因为 token 数本身正是被比较对象。\n\n词表规模方面，BPE 和 parity-aware BPE 尽量与 MYTE 对齐。MYTE 以每语言 4096 / 8192 / 12288 morphemes 三档构建，总词表分别约 45k / 90k / 135k。BPE 系列匹配这些总规模。BLT 没有固定词表，因此通过控制平均 patch size（4.5 / 6 / 8 bytes）来近似比较。真正训练 1.5B decoder-only LM 时，作者选用 90k 规模 tokenizer；BLT 则采用平均 4.5 bytes patch 的配置。\n\n方法上的亮点不在于提出新 tokenizer，而在于把 intrinsic tokenizer analysis 和 downstream LM training 连起来。前者看压缩效率和跨语言公平性，后者看在相同训练语料和近似计算预算下，tokenizer 差异是否会转化为语言建模能力差异。尤其值得注意的是，作者还把 tokenizer 训练和数据 tokenization 的硬件成本单独列出：MYTE 和 BPE 系列可在 CPU 上完成，而 BLT 的 entropy tokenizer 训练和大规模 tokenization 需要多张 H100/H200。这让比较不只停留在理论 token 长度，而是延伸到系统可部署性。","experiments":"实验覆盖 11 种东南亚语言：English、Burmese、Chinese、Indonesian、Khmer、Lao、Malay、Tagalog、Tamil、Thai、Vietnamese。论文先做 tokenizer 层面的 intrinsic evaluation，再训练 1.5B decoder-only LM 做下游比较。\n\n从给定正文可以确认几组关键数字。Tokenizer 训练集是 100 万句、3.5GB；LM 训练集是 1 亿句、203GB。正式训练的 tokenizer 规模选 90k，总体接近主流 LLM 的 100k–128k 词表。训练硬件为 4–8 张 H100/H200，并把训练时长折算到 8×H200 等效小时。作者明确指出：MYTE 的训练成本最高，达到 300 normalized hours，原因是它切分后 token 总数显著增加，训练语料达到 269B tokens；Byte-level BPE 最省。BLT 的 tokenizer 训练和数据处理成本也明显更高，因为需要 GPU 上的 entropy model 和 patching pipeline。\n\n从论文主旨推断，结果不会是单边压倒式结论，而是 trade-off：公平化 tokenizer 在低资源、非拉丁语言上改善压缩率和 token parity，但不同方法的收益位置不同。Parity-aware BPE 更像低改造成本的折中方案；MYTE 可能在语言学结构上更细致，但代价是 token 数暴涨、训练更慢；BLT 试图绕开固定词表偏置，但系统复杂度和预处理成本更高。作者强调 controlled comparison，因此真正值得看的不是单一平均分，而是各语言 token length disparity、训练 token 总量、以及下游任务上不同语言的能力变化是否一致。","reading_guide":"这篇适合做 tokenizer 方向的泛读到半精读。先看 Introduction，把问题框定清楚：为什么 byte-level BPE 会天然偏向拉丁脚本，为什么这会影响多语言 LLM 的 compute allocation。然后直接看 Methods，重点确认三件事：训练数据是否统一、词表规模是否可比、BLT 这种非固定词表方法是如何被纳入同一比较框架的。结果部分建议优先看两类表：一类是 intrinsic tokenizer 指标，另一类是 1.5B LM 的下游表现和训练成本表。尤其要留意 token count 与性能的关系，而不是只看最终任务分数。\n\n预计阅读时间 30–45 分钟。如果你做 multilingual pretrain，值得进一步深挖两个问题：其一，公平 tokenizer 带来的 token 膨胀是否可以通过更好的 data mixture 或 per-language loss weighting 抵消；其二，词表公平性和 scaling efficiency 是否存在稳定的 Pareto 前沿。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a basic but often under-controlled issue in multilingual LLMs: tokenization bias systematically inflates sequence length for non-Latin and lower-resource languages, which then affects training efficiency, inference cost, usable context length, and cross-lingual capability. Prior work proposed alternatives such as parity-aware BPE, morphology-driven tokenization, and BLT-style byte patching, but these methods were usually evaluated against byte-level BPE in isolation. The central question here is not whether one new tokenizer can beat BPE, but what each tokenizer family buys and costs under a controlled multilingual setup: compression, equity, downstream modeling quality, and system efficiency.","importance":"For pretraining research, this is directly relevant. It treats the tokenizer as a first-order component of the training recipe rather than a preprocessing detail. Different tokenizers change not only sequence length but also how much effective training signal each language receives under a fixed compute budget. The paper also makes “equity” operational by measuring tokenization disparity across languages instead of discussing fairness only at a high level. That matters for multilingual scaling, data mixture design, and context allocation. Just as importantly, the study highlights a practical trade-off: a more equitable tokenizer may improve parity while worsening total token count, training time, hardware cost, or implementation complexity.","technical_approach":"$4","experiments":"$5","reading_guide":"This is worth a careful skim, and a deeper read if you work on multilingual pretraining. (","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T22:56:12Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2606_15007.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是大模型在 tokens 的 fresh GitHub code 数据，以及 specialized synthetic pretraining data 和 post-training data。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Pretraining 部分，尤其是 architecture、NVFP4 recipe、data mixture、training instability We propos","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T20:39:33Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14943.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 causal Transformer 不能直接建模任意条件分布的问题。标准 GPT 可以高效计算 p(x_{>t}|x_{≤t})，但如果条件集合来自未来、两侧上下文或任意位置，例如文本 infilling、局部改写、在已知结论约束下补全中间推理，直接用自回归分解会遇到不可 tractable 的边缘化问题。论文目标不是另做一个 BERT 式去噪模型，而是在保留 GPT 左到右建模能力的同时，让同一个 decoder-only Transformer 能单次前向计算和采样 p(x_e|x_c)。","importance":"这篇论文的价值在于把“任意条件语言建模”拉回到标准 causal LM 的工程路径上。它没有引入复杂的新架构，而是通过条件 token 复制和 attention mask 设计，让 GPT 同时支持传统 next-token likelihood 和任意条件 likelihood。对预训练研究的影响主要有三点：第一，它提供了一种比 masked LM 更接近自回归链式分解的 infilling 训练目标；第二，它说明 arbitrary conditional 并不一定要牺牲左到右生成质量；第三，它给现有 LLM 增加未来上下文条件能力提供了可微调路径，而不是从头训练专用模型。","technical_approach":"ac-gpt 的核心做法很简单：给定原序列 x，将 token 分为 conditioning set x_c 和 evaluation set x_e。模型只对 x_e 中的 token 计算预测损失，x_c 因为已知，不能纳入 loss。为了让所有待预测 token 都能看到任意位置的条件 token，方法把 x_c 复制一份放到序列“开头”，记为 \\bar{x}_c。这些复制 token 在 attention 顺序上位于最前面，因此后续 token 都能 attend 到它们；但它们保留原始位置编码，所以模型知道条件 token 在原文中的位置。复制的条件 token 之间使用双向 attention，因为它们都已观测，不存在泄漏问题。evaluation token 仍然沿原始顺序使用 causal attention，并按左到右链式分解计算联合概率。\n\n关键细节是为什么必须复制条件 token，而不是直接允许所有 token attend 到原序列里的 x_c。论文指出，如果直接开放对原始条件 token 的 attention，多层 Transformer 会产生间接信息泄漏。例如 x_3 是条件 token，x_1、x_2、x_4 是待评估 token。x_3 为了预测 x_4 需要看到 x_1、x_2；但下一层中 x_1 又可能通过 x_3 间接获得 x_2 信息，破坏 p(x_e|x_c) 内部左到右分解。复制条件 token 能把“已知上下文”和“原序列自回归状态”分离开，避免这种跨层泄漏。\n\n与 BERT/denoising LM 的区别在于，ac-gpt 不是独立预测 masked token，也不是用伪似然近似联合分布。它仍然保留 evaluation tokens 的自回归链式建模，因此可以给 arbitrary conditional 一个一致的 joint likelihood。与需要改架构的任意顺序模型相比，它更接近现有 GPT 训练和推理栈，微调现有 LLM 的成本也更低。","experiments":"论文报告了三类核心结果。第一，在多种 conditional likelihood 评测中，ac-gpt 优于现有 arbitrary conditional baseline，并且没有明显损害标准 left-to-right language modeling。这个结果很关键，因为许多 bidirectional 或 的可行性，说明这种能力可以作为模型能力扩展，而不一定要求重新预训练。\n\n从给定内容能确认的实现细节包括：模型基于 GPT-2 风格 decoder-only Transformer，位置编码使用 RoPE；训练时随机构造 conditioning/evaluation partition，loss 只作用在 evaluation set；conditioning copies 保留原始位置编码，并在条件副本内部使用非因果 attention。给定内容没有包含完整实验表格，无法可靠给出具体参数规模、数据量和各 benchmark 的精确数值。","reading_guide":"建议精读方法部分和 Figure 1。重点看三个点：一是 conditioning token copy 的构造方式；二是 copy token 为什么保留原始位置编码；三是防止多层 attention 间接泄漏的例子。这个例子是理解论文的关键。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 conditional likelihood、infilling/text editing sampling、left-to-right LM 保持能力这三组结果。如果有消融表，重点看去掉 copy mechanism、改用原始条件 token attention、去掉条件副本内部双向 attention、改变 partition 策略后的差异。真正值得深挖的是训练分布设计：conditioning set 的采样比例、span/block 条件与随机 token 条件的混合、以及这种 objective 是否能在大规模 continued pretraining 中稳定提升 infilling 和 reasoning-to-known-target 能力。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a limitation of causal Transformers: they can efficiently model left-to-right conditionals such as p(x_{>t}|x_{≤t}), but they cannot tractably evaluate or sample arbitrary conditionals p(x_e|x_c) when the observed tokens may come from the future, from both sides, or from an arbitrary subset of positions. The target use cases include infilling, local text editing, and generation under a known conclusion. mask diffusion language/model family 里一个很核心但一直没被真正打开的能力缺口：模型虽然天生支持 masked token 的并行重采样，但现有解码基本还是单轮、被动、吸收式的，一旦某个 token 被“确认”，后面很难主动回头改。这样它并没有把 diffusion 范式相对 AR 的潜在优势用出来。作者要做的不是再造一个更强的 denoiser，而是让 MDM 学会在生成过程中自己判断哪里不可靠、哪里值得重遮盖，再做多轮局部修正，把 reasoning 从“向前续写”改成“对当前状态反复修订”。","importance":"对新型语言建模范式研究，这篇属于应当认真看的工作。原因有三点。第一，它抓住了 MDM 与 AR 最本质的机制差异：AR 的 test-time scaling 主要靠延长推理轨迹和重生成，MDM 则有机会把额外算力投到局部修正上。第二，它不是只在 decoding 上做启发式 remask，而是通过轻量 post-training 把 reflective masking 训练成模型内生能力，这对 diffusion LM 是否能形成独立 reasoning 路线很关键。第三，它提出的 History Reference 给了一个很实用的方向：MDM 缺少 AR 那种天然的“历史上下文串联”，于是需要显式保留去噪轨迹中的中间状态，给后续修正提供时间维度上的记忆。对 pretrain 研究员来说，这篇的意义不在于它已经给出最终答案，而在于它把“mask-based model 的 reasoning 应该如何定义和训练”讲清楚了。","technical_approach":"技术上有两个主件。第一是 Reflective Masking。作者把每个位置、每个 denoising step 的动作定义成三选一：保留当前 token、把当前 token 重新 mask 掉等待重预测、或者对当前 mask 位置生成新 token。关键不是固定 remask 比例，而是根据模型当前的不确定性做逐位置决策，让模型能主动推翻先前已经 denoise 的内容。这样生成过程从单调收敛变成可逆的局部修订过程。第二是 History Reference。它不引入额外可学习参数，而是把先前若干步的中间预测状态聚合成 history-aware embedding，让模型在当前 step 不只看“现在的部分序列”，还看“这个位置之前被预测成过什么、改过几次、稳定不稳定”。这相当于给 MDM 补上一条 AR 模型天然拥有、但 diffusion 解码里通常缺失的时间记忆通道。\n\n训练部分是全文最值得细看的地方。作者强调不能只靠推理时加规则，因为模型原本并没有学过多轮自发修订。于是他们设计了轻量 post-training，把 reflective masking 作为中心能力来训，并配套一个数据构造流程，生成与模型原生输出分布更对齐的训练样本。这里的关键取舍是：如果直接用人工构造错误或离分布 corruption，模型学到的可能只是修补特定噪声；作者更希望它学会在自身生成轨迹里识别不可靠 token 并修正，因此训练信号要贴近模型真实会犯的错。这个思路和很多 post-train reasoning 工作里“用模型自己的 rollout 造纠错数据”是同一脉络，但在 MDM 上更自然，因为 mask/re-mask 本来就是原生操作。","experiments":"实验覆盖图像编辑、Sudoku、文本生成三类任务，目的是证明这不是某个单一模态或强监督场景下的技巧。全文给出的主结论很稳定：Reflective Masking 在不同任务上都优于不具备多轮反思修订能力的基线 MDM；当任务需要模型自主探索、自己发现哪里错了时，History Reference 的增益更明显。图像编辑场景里，因为指令同时给了修改位置和修改目标，RM 主要体现为更稳定的局部编辑和更少的无关区域破坏；Sudoku 里，它体现为能反复定位冲突格并局部修正，而不是整盘重来；文本生成里，由于监督最弱、没有直接提示最终答案，RM 更能体现“通过迭代修订逼近正确输出”的能力。摘要和正文片段没有给出足够完整的数值表，因此不宜编造具体提升幅度；但从作者组织实验的方式看，重点不只是最终分数，而是证明 RM 带来的 test-time scaling 是一种不同于 AR CoT 的计算分配方式：额外步数主要用于 selective revision，而不是线性扩展输出长度。","reading_guide":"这篇建议精读。先看 Introduction，抓住作者的核心立场：MDM 的 reasoning 优势不该理解为并行生成更快，而该理解为可以对中间状态做显式局部修订。然后直接读第 3 节 Reflective Masking，重点看三部分：逐位置 keep/re-mask/reveal 决策规则、History Reference 的构造方式、训练目标与训练样本如何生成。这里决定了方法是不是一个临时 decoding trick，还是一个可迁移的能力激活方案。接着看实验部分时，不要只盯最终指标，更要看不同任务中 RM 起作用的机制是否一致：是靠修正低置信 token，还是靠利用历史轨迹避免重复犯错。若时间有限，优先看方法图、算法描述、Sudoku 和文本任务结果，因为这两类最能体现 reasoning 与 self-correction。值得深挖的点有三个：一，反射式重遮盖是否能在更大语言模型上形成稳定 scaling；二，History Reference 能否与外部 memory 或 verifier 结合；三，这种 post-training 能力是否应前移到 pretraining，让 MDM 从一开始就学会可逆生成和局部修订。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a central limitation of mask diffusion models: although they can resample masked positions in parallel, standard decoding is still largely one-pass and absorbing. Once a token is confidently denoised, it is effectively fixed, so the model cannot actively revisit earlier commitments. This prevents MDMs from exploiting their main structural advantage over autoregressive models. The paper’s goal is to turn generation from monotonic forward completion into iterative state refinement, where the model can decide which parts of its current output are unreliable and should be re-masked and revised.","importance":"For researchers interested in alternative language-modeling paradigms, this is one of the more meaningful directions. It matters for three reasons. First, it identifies a genuine mechanism-level distinction between AR and mask-based generation: AR test-time scaling mostly spends extra compute on longer trajectories or regeneration, whereas MDMs could spend it on local revision. Second, the paper does not stop at heuristic remasking during inference; it uses lightweight post-training to make reflective masking an internalized capability. That is important if diffusion-style LMs are to develop a reasoning path that is not just an imitation of AR CoT. During denoising, each MDLM tracks confidence dynamics over answer-relevant positions rather than relying on a single-step confidence score. The model with the more stable trajectory is treated as the more reliable lead at that stage. Models with unstable trajectories receive partially denoised sequences from the more reliable model and continue generation from those intermediate states. This exchange repeats periodically, allowing the leading model to change over the denoising process. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-15T07:56:05Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_16360.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 latent reasoning 里一个一直没有被真正解决的问题：模型不只是“能不能插入 latent token”，而是要在自回归解码过程中，在线决定三件事——什么时候进入 latent computation、进入后做哪一类计算、以及分配多少计算预算。已有方法大多把 latent reasoning 当成一种固定接口：要么预先规定 latent step 出现的位置，要么只做二元决策，判断是否触发 latent mode。这样做的问题是，latent computation 与当前推理状态耦合不够，也无法表达不同推理阶段需要不同内部计算的事实。Tyler 把这个问题重新表述为 typed and budgeted computation：把隐式推理看成若干具有明确功能角色的算子，由模型在生成时按需调用，而不是把所有隐式推理都压进一种统一的连续表示里。","importance":"对预训练和基座建模的启发主要有两层。第一，它把“latent reasoning 是否有用”推进到“latent reasoning 应该如何被组织和调度”。这比单纯压缩 CoT 更接近一种新的语言建模接口：visible token 负责对外通信，latent operator 负责内部计算。第二，它强调 latent computation 的功能分化，而不是把 latent token 当成无类型的软提示或隐藏状态补丁。这一点对未来把 reasoning 作为模型内生计算资源来训练很关键，因为它涉及计算分配、推理阶段结构、以及跨任务迁移时哪些内部操作可以复用。就量级看，这还不是替代 AR 预训练范式的工作，但它给出了一个比较完整的 online latent reasoning 框架，尤其对“test-time compute 如何由模型自己调度”这个方向有方法论价值。","technical_approach":"$e","experiments":"全文给出的实验重点是三类：多骨干、多任务泛化，以及连续适应。摘要和正文可确认的结论是，Tyler 在多个 backbone 和多个 reasoning benchmark 上都优于强基线，而且提升能迁移到训练域之外。作者特别强调两点分析结果。\n\n第一，policy 学到了与任务难度和推理阶段相关的 operator 使用模式。较容易样本或较直接的生成阶段，更倾向于继续 visible decoding；难度上升时，latent operator 调用频率增加。不同 operator 的触发位置也不同：Og 更像前期的全局定向，Os 更像中间过程中的状态修正，Op 更偏向需要复用程序化解题模板的阶段。这说明 typed design 不是纯粹的人为命名，至少在行为上形成了可分化的功能角色。\n\n第二，在 sequential adaptation 设置里，作者按 code、science、math、theorem reasoning 这样的顺序做连续适应，Tyler 的最终性能更高，同时遗忘更低。这个结果对基座研究更有意思，因为它暗示 typed latent operators 可能提供了一种比纯显式 CoT 更稳的内部计算接口：新领域适应时，不必完全改写外显推理分布，而是部分复用已有 operator 的内部功能。\n\n从你给出的全文片段里，具体参数量、数据规模、各 benchmark 的绝对分数和提升幅度没有完整展开，因此不适合编造数字。阅读时应重点去正文实验表确认：1）用了哪些 backbone；2）训练域与测试域是否严格分离；3）相对 fixed latent、untyped latent、纯 CoT baseline 的提升幅度；4）budget 分配的消融是否证明“typed + adaptive budget”同时必要，而不是只靠更多 test-time compute 获益。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读后挑重点深看，预计 35 到 50 分钟。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 2，确认作者到底把 latent reasoning 重新定义成了什么问题。很多类似工作只是换一种 soft token 接口，这篇的关键在于 online typed scheduling。\n\n方法部分重点看三处。第一，三类 operator 的形式化定义，以及 latent synthesis module 如何把 operator 与当前状态映射成 latent token 序列。这里决定了 typed latent 是否真的有独立参数化和独立功能。第二，policy 的动作空间设计，尤其 budget 是离散选择还是连续参数化，奖励如何构造，GRPO 在这里优化的对象到底是单独 policy head 还是与主模型共享部分参数。第三，operator-anchored auxiliary objective 的具体形式，因为这关系到稀疏调用场景下 RL 是否稳定。\n\n实验部分优先看：主结果表、operator ablation、budget ablation、跨域泛化、sequential adaptation。若时间有限，可以跳过常规 benchmark 描述，直接看能否回答三个问题：typed operator 是否比 untyped latent 更好；adaptive invocation 是否比 fixed schedule 更好；continual adaptation 的收益是否来自更少遗忘而不只是更高初始分数。\n\n如果要深挖，最值得追的是两个点。一个是 typed operator 是否真的学到可迁移的“计算原语”，还是仍然高度依赖训练任务分布。另一个是这种框架能否前移到 pretraining 或 mid-training，而不只是 reasoning finetune 阶段使用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a missing piece in latent reasoning: not merely how to insert latent tokens, but how to let an autoregressive LM decide online when to invoke latent computation, what kind of internal computation to run, and how much budget to spend. Prior latent-reasoning methods usually hard-code the invocation pattern or reduce the decision to a binary trigger. That leaves the model with little control over how latent computation should interact with the evolving reasoning state. Tyler reframes the problem as typed, budgeted computation during decoding, where latent reasoning is treated as an internal compute interface rather than a compressed form of chain-of-thought.","importance":"Its main value is conceptual. We evaluate this approach a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-15T15:08:09Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_16825.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"MoE架构通过每token仅激活少量专家FFN，实现了总参数量与每token计算量的解耦，成为大模型扩展的主流方案；但当前开源MoE的每token激活参数占比仅3%-10%，训练和推理时仍需加载全部专家参数，显存几乎被闲置参数主导，这与近期追求“单参数计算效率最大化”的趋势（如循环深度模型、OpenAI Parameter Golf挑战）直接矛盾。此外，现有MoE跨层参数共享工作未通过控制变量实验系统分析各组件（专家FFN、路由、注意力、归一化）共享的质量代价，无法指导最优共享策略设计。","importance":"提供了可直接落地的MoE参数效率优化方案，无需修改现有训练/推理基础设施即可大幅降低显存占用。通过系统的控制变量实验厘清了MoE各组件跨层共享的质量代价差异，为后续参数高效MoE、循环深度架构设计提供了实证依据。提出的异构宽度扩展思路将专家绑定从单纯的压缩技术，拓展为MoE深度-宽度权衡的新设计维度。","technical_approach":"核心方案为Expert Tying（专家绑定）：将连续g层Transformer中所有MoE专家FFN的gate/up/down投影参数通过Python级指针别名实现跨层共享，仅需优化器正确处理绑定参数在多个使用位置的梯度累积，无需修改其他训练/推理基础设施；同时保留每层独立的路由、注意力投影与RMSNorm参数，确保隐藏状态仍经过g个不同的层算子变换。通过控制变量消融验证了该方案的可行性：每层独立的注意力是维持层间差异的核心（而非路由），因此共享参数量最大的专家FFN的质量代价极低，而共享注意力的质量损失会高出一个数量级。补充两个关键设计规则：1）模型首尾各2层不参与绑定（2+2的非绑定首尾段），该设计带来最大的单架构性能增益；2）异构宽度扩展：将专家绑定节省的参数重新投入到中间绑定层，增加专家数量，可在同参数量下稳定超过未绑定基线。此前的MoE跨层共享研究未开展跨架构、多粒度的控制变量分析，本工作的系统实验覆盖了这一缺口。","experiments":"实验分为两阶段：1）小模型控制实验：采用32层解码器-only Transformer（d_model=512，8注意力头，序列长512，32专家top-8激活）验证组件共享代价；2）大规模预训练实验：覆盖OLMoE、Qwen3-MoE、DeepSeekMoE三种主流生产级MoE架构，最大参数量达7B。核心结果：当绑定组大小g=4时，总参数量最多降低52%，验证困惑度与下游任务精度几乎无退化，训练 wall-clock 速度最高提升23.7%。组件消融显示：解绑注意力带来的loss改善幅度比仅解绑路由高出一个数量级，验证了注意力是维持层差异的核心；异构宽度扩展方案在三种架构、同参数量设置下均超过未绑定基线；2+2非绑定首尾段的设计带来消融实验中最大的单架构性能增益。","reading_guide":"泛读优先看Introduction部分对MoE显存开销与参数效率趋势矛盾的问题定义，再重点阅读Section 3的组件消融实验（核心结论：注意力是维持层差异的核心，专家FFN可安全共享，首尾层不宜绑定），以及Section 4的跨架构大规模预训练结果，尤其是异构宽度扩展的实验设计。预计阅读时间30-40分钟，重点关注组件消融的对比图表与跨架构性能对比表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"MoE architectures decouple total parameter count from per-token compute by activating only a small subset of expert FFNs per token, but require storing all expert parameters in memory during training and inference. evaluation cost grows linearly with the number of languages, translation errors are hard to detect at scale, and some items conflate general capability with culture-/language-specific knowledge, distorting cross-lingual comparisons. The paper addresses all three by extending Item Response Theory (IRT) to explicitly model (item, model, language) interactions and to separate content effects from language effects.","importance":"The main value for pretraining teams is in the evaluation/data loop rather than model architecture:\n- Multilingual pretraining and continual pretraining often require fast iteration on language mixture and data cleaning; exhaustive evaluation across all languages is a bottleneck. A partial-eval → full-estimate approach can reduce iteration latency.\n- Per-language difficulty shifts provide a systematic way to surface translation issues, improving benchmark hygiene and potentially informing training-data QA.\n- Splitting discriminability into content vs language components helps diagnose whether a benchmark is measuring reasoning/knowledge or language proficiency/translation artifacts, preventing misguided data-mixture decisions.","technical_approach":"The method extends 2PL IRT to multilingual parallel structure by modeling correctness for each (item i, model j, language l):\n- Language-specific item difficulty deviation d_{i,l}: allows the same item to be harder/easier in different languages, capturing translation and phrasing effects.\n- Model language ability residual ε_{j,l}: captures how model j’s proficiency in language l deviates from its overall ability θ_j.\n- Discriminability split: decomposes item discriminability into a language-invariant content component a_i^{base} and a language-dependent component a_i^{lang} tied to the language residual dimension. Intuitively, some items separate models mainly by language proficiency rather than content.\nFitted parameters are then used for: (1) predicting unobserved (i,j,l) to enable efficient evaluation, (2) ranking large |d_{i,l}| to flag translation errors, and (3) using a^{lang}/a^{base} to identify culture-/language-specific items.","experiments":"They fit Multilingual-IRT on MMLU-Pro-X predictions from 25 LLMs across 29 languages. Key findings:\n- For predicting unseen (item, model, language) instances under various subsampling rates, Multilingual-IRT reduces binary cross-entropy by ~11–16% versus the strongest accuracy-based baseline.\n- Ranking by per-language difficulty shifts d_{i,l} surfaces candidate translation errors across all 28 non-English languages more evenly, whereas accuracy-based heuristics concentrate detections in only a few languages.\n- Ranking by discriminability ratio a^{lang}/a^{base} recovers culture-specific items that accuracy-based statistics miss.\n(The excerpt provided does not include all per-language numeric breakdowns; if your multilingual dashboard is still mean accuracy, this should change it."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Google Research","The Hebrew University of Jerusalem"],"authors":["Gili Lior","Tzviel Frostig","Gabriel Stanovsky","Matan Eyal"],"notable_authors":["Gabriel Stanovsky"],"tags":["multilingual-evaluation","item-response-theory","benchmark-diagnostics","mmlu-pro-x"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15643","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.15643","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.16151","title":"GRACE: Step-Level Benchmark for Faithful Reasoning over Context","abstract":"Many reasoning tasks require models to reason over input context, from document-grounded question answering to rule-based deduction. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting produces traces that appear transparent, yet individual steps can silently deviate from the source evidence, even when the final answer is correct. Existing methods detect hallucinations at the response level but fail to identify where in the chain a failure occurs or what type it is. We introduce GRACE, the first human-annotated CoT 过程是否逐步忠实于证据，而不是只看最终答案对不对。现有 hallucination 或 faithfulness 评测大多停留在 response level，最多判断整段回答有没有问题，无法定位是哪一步开始偏离上下文，也无法区分偏离是逻辑跳步、约束违背，还是凭空捏造证据。论文把这个问题形式化为 step-level context faithfulness：给定 references、question 和 reasoning trace，对每一步判断 faithful / unfaithful，并在出错时给出错误类别和解释。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对 post-train 信号设计、reasoning 数据构造、过程监督和 verifier 训练有明显价值。它把“过程是否忠实于上下文”从一个模糊概念变成了可标注、可训练、可评测的对象。更重要的是，作者明确区分了 process faithfulness 和 context faithfulness：一个步骤可以真实反映模型内部推理，但仍然不被上下文支持；也可以表面上贴合上下文，却未必是模型真实决策路径。这个区分对做 reasoning RL、step-level reward、judge/verifier 的人很关键，因为你到底在优化哪种信号，会直接影响训练结果。","technical_approach":"方法核心是构建 GRACE benchmark，并配套一个数据驱动的错误 taxonomy。数据覆盖 10 个模型、4 个源数据集、2 类推理场景：evidence-grounded reasoning 和 deductive reasoning。每条 CoT 的每一步都标注三类信息：faithfulness label、error category、自然语言解释。\n\ntaxonomy 不是先验手工定义，而是 bottom-up 发现。作者先让强 LLM judge 对大量 unfaithful steps 生成自由文本 critique，再把这些 critique 做 embedding 和聚类，归纳出两条经验上可分的错误主线：GRACE-Inference 和 GRACE-Grounding。前者更偏推理错误，如无依据跳步、约束违背、无效演绎；后者更偏证据错误，如事实矛盾、幻觉证据、抽取错误。这个设计的好处是 taxonomy 来自真实错误分布，而不是研究者预设标签。\n\n数据构建上，作者采用多 LLM judges 先做大规模自动标注。多个 judge 一致同意的样本进入可扩展训练集；judge 分歧较大的复杂样本交给人工标注，形成高质量 evaluation set。这样做是在成本和质量之间取平衡：训练集追求规模，测试集追求难度和可靠性。论文还把这个 benchmark 用在两个下游方向：一是训练 step-level evaluator，小模型微调后可以接近大模型；二是把 context faithfulness 信号接入 RL，作为下游任务训练中的额外奖励或约束，观察对准确率和推理可靠性的影响。","experiments":"从全文给出的结果看，最重要的不是某个单点分数，而是三类发现。第一，GRACE evaluation set 对现有模型仍有明显 headroom。也就是说，即便最终答案正确，step-level 上仍有相当比例的 unfaithful reasoning；反过来，完全 faithful 的链条也可能得到错误答案。这说明 answer accuracy 不能替代过程忠实性评测。\n\n第二，taxonomy 的区分是有经验支持的。通过对大量错误步骤的 critique 聚类，作者发现 inference-type 和 grounding-type 错误在分布上是可分的，而不是一个混杂标签。这让 evaluator 不只是输出“错了”，还能指出错在推理还是错在证据绑定。\n\n第三，训练信号层面有两个结果。其一，用 GRACE 训练集微调较小 evaluator，可以达到接近更大模型的性能，说明 step-level faithfulness 判断是可以被蒸馏和专门学习的，而不必完全依赖最强 judge。其二，把 step-level context faithfulness 信号并入 RL 训练后，下游任务准确率和 reasoning reliability 都有提升。摘要和引言没有给出完整数字，全文主实验更值得看具体表格：重点不是绝对提升幅度，而是这种 reward 没有只把模型推向更保守的短回答，而是在正确率和忠实性上同时受益。\n\n需要注意的是，这篇论文的实验重心是 benchmark 与 evaluator，而不是提出新的推理模型架构。因此如果从 pretrain 视角阅读，最该关注的是它如何定义可学习的过程监督信号，而不是把它当成又一个 CoT 数据集。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和 Task Formulation，吃透 context faithfulness 与 process faithfulness 的区别；这是全文最重要的概念边界。然后看 taxonomy 构建部分，尤其是自由文本 critique 如何聚类成 GRACE-Inference / GRACE-Grounding，这决定了 benchmark 是否只是人工拍脑袋分标签。实验部分优先看三块：不同模型在 evaluation set 上的 headroom、small evaluator fine-tuning 的结果、以及把 faithfulness 信号接入 RL 后的变化。如果时间有限，40 分钟左右可以抓住主线。泛读时不必逐条看 annotation guideline，但值得留意作者如何处理 judge disagreement，因为这关系到数据噪声和后续可训练性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific gap in reasoning evaluation: whether each step in a context-grounded chain of thought is faithful to the provided evidence, rather than merely whether the final answer is correct. Existing hallucination and faithfulness methods mostly operate at the response level. They can flag that something went wrong, but they usually cannot localize the failure to a particular reasoning step or distinguish between unsupported inference, constraint violation, and fabricated evidence. The paper formalizes this as step-level context faithfulness: given references, a question, and a reasoning trace, label each step as faithful or unfaithful, and assign an error type plus explanation when it is unfaithful.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for post-training signal design, process supervision, verifier training, and reasoning data construction. Its main contribution is to turn context faithfulness from a vague desideratum into something that can be annotated, benchmarked, and optimized. A particularly useful conceptual move is the separation between process faithfulness and context faithfulness. A step may reflect the model's actual internal reasoning while still being unsupported by the context, or it may look well-grounded without reflecting the true decision process. That distinction matters if one wants to build RL rewards, step-level judges, or verifier models without conflating different notions of correctness.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"$16","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and task formulation. The distinction between context faithfulness and process faithfulness is the conceptual anchor of the paper. Then read the taxonomy construction section, especially how free-form critiques are clustered into GRACE-Inference and GRACE-Grounding. That part determines whether the benchmark is genuinely data-driven or just manually relabeled. In the experiments, prioritize three pieces: model headroom on the human evaluation set, fine-tuning results for smaller evaluators, and the RL experiments that use faithfulness as a training signal. If reading quickly, about 40 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas. In our setup, an agent should uncover a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA) by interacting with an oracle through (1) membership queries (\"Does this string belong to the target language?\") and (2) equivalence queries (\"Is this the target DFA?\"). LLM agent 在与环境交互时，能否真正学出一个可验证的世界模型，而不是只靠局部模式和短视试探。作者把问题落到 active automata learning 上，让模型通过 membership query 和 equivalence query 去识别一个隐藏 DFA。这样做的好处是，环境结构已知、复杂度可控、最优或强基线算法现成，因而可以把“会不会建模环境”拆成可测的几个维度：成功率、查询效率、随状态数增长的退化曲线，以及交互轨迹中的规划失误类型。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，但对“agent 能力到底来自哪里”给了一个很干净的诊断框架。价值主要有三层。第一，它把 world-model inference 从开放任务里抽离出来，避免 benchmark 污染和任务提示技巧，把能力测成一个受控变量。第二，它说明当前强推理模型即便在小规模离散环境里也远未接近经典算法，问题不只是知识不足，而是主动查询规划、证据整合、假设修正这几步都不稳。第三，这类结果对预训练和后训练都有启发：如果模型在这种最小化环境建模任务上都明显掉队，那么仅靠更长 CoT 或更大模型未必能补齐，可能需要更明确的交互式训练信号、外部记忆、程序化状态表示，或把“查询策略学习”直接纳入训练目标。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者把经典 automata learning 重新包装成 agentic setting。每个任务实例先采样一个目标 DFA，agent 只能通过两类工具与 oracle 交互：MQ 询问某个字符串是否被目标语言接受，EQ 提交一个候选 DFA，若错误则得到反例。评测时既看最终是否恢复正确 DFA，也看用了多少查询。复杂度主要由最小 DFA 的状态数控制。基线选了经典的 L* 和 TTT，这一点很关键，因为它让结果不只是“模型彼此比较”，而是“模型与已知高效学习程序比较”。从全文描述看，作者还做了轨迹级分析，把失败分成几类：查询不够信息化、对已有观测推理不一致、构造假设时没有正确利用历史证据、收到 成功率，而经典算法在同类任务上是 100%。第二，即便只看成功样本，最强模型 Gemini 3.1 Pro 的查询数仍比 TTT 多约 45.8%，说明问题不只是偶尔失败，而是整体发现过程低效。第三，reasoning model 与非 reasoning model 差距很大：在 4–5 状态任务上，Gemini 3.1 Pro 可达 85% 成功，而 GPT-5.4 非 reasoning、Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite、Llama-3.3-70B 为 0%。这些数字说明，显式推理能力确实有帮助，但帮助主要体现在把模型从“几乎不会做”拉到“能做一部分”，离稳定、系统化的环境归纳还很远。轨迹分析部分的价值也不小：模型常提出冗余或低区分度查询，不能围绕当前 hypothesis 的不确定边界去设计 probe；拿到反例后，也常不能把反例转成结构性修正，而只是局部打补丁。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和任务定义，理解为什么 DFA learning 是一个比一般 agent benchmark 更干净的世界模型测试。然后重点看实验主表和按状态数分层的结果图，抓住两个事实：性能随复杂度的退化斜率，以及与 L*/TTT 的效率差。接着看 trajectory analysis，尤其是作者如何把失败拆成 query planning、evidence integration、hypothesis construction 三类。若只花 20–30 分钟，建议读任务设定、主结果、失败案例三部分即可。若要深挖，可以思考两件事：一是当前 agent 框架里，模型到底缺的是搜索策略、外部记忆，还是离散结构归纳偏置；二是如果把这类任务做成 post-training 或 RL 环境，奖励该如何设计，才能学到接近经典 learner 的查询策略。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question: can tool-using LLM agents actually infer a latent world model through interaction, rather than relying on shallow local heuristics? It instantiates this as active automata learning: the agent must recover a hidden DFA using membership queries and equivalence queries. This setup gives controlled environment complexity, exact ground truth, strong algorithmic baselines, and trajectory-level observability, so “world-model inference” becomes measurable instead of anecdotal.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for foundation-model researchers because it isolates one capability that many agent benchmarks blur together. The contribution is mainly diagnostic. First, it turns latent-structure inference into a clean controlled testbed. Second, it shows that current reasoning-capable LLM agents remain far from classical learners even in small deterministic environments, so the gap is not just missing knowledge but weak query planning, weak evidence aggregation, and unstable hypothesis revision. Third, it suggests that scaling CoT alone is unlikely to close this gap; more explicit interactive training signals, external state tracking, or policy learning over information-gathering actions may be needed.","technical_approach":"The method recasts classical active automata learning as an agentic evaluation environment. For each instance, an oracle is initialized with a target DFA. The LLM agent can call two tools: MQ asks whether a string is accepted by the target language, and EQ submits a hypothesized DFA, which is either accepted or rejected with a counterexample. Task difficulty is controlled by the number of states in the minimal DFA. Evaluation measures both final identification success and interaction efficiency in number of queries. A strong design choice is the inclusion of classical learners such as L* and TTT as baselines, which anchors the results against known efficient procedures rather than only against other LLMs. The paper also goes beyond aggregate scores and analyzes trajectories, attributing failures to poor query informativeness, inconsistent reasoning over accumulated evidence, weak use of prior observations when constructing hypotheses, and ineffective use of counterexamples for refinement.","experiments":"Three results matter most. First, performance drops sharply as DFA size grows: for 9-state automata, no tested model exceeds 25% success, while classical algorithms solve all instances. Second, even on successful runs, Gemini 3.1 Pro, the best model in the study, uses about 45.8% more queries than TTT, indicating a substantial efficiency gap. Third, reasoning models are much stronger than non-reasoning ones: on 4–5 state automata, Gemini 3.1 Pro reaches 85% success, whereas GPT-5.4 without reasoning, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, and Llama-3.3-70B score 0%. The trajectory analysis is equally informative: models often ask redundant or weakly discriminative queries, fail to maintain a coherent global hypothesis, and treat counterexamples as local patches rather than structural evidence for DFA refinement.","reading_guide":"Start with the task formulation and motivation sections to see why DFA learning is a cleaner probe of world-model inference than open-ended agent benchmarks. Then read the main results tables and the plots stratified by DFA size; the key signal is the degradation slope with complexity and the efficiency gap to L*/TTT. After that, focus on the trajectory analysis and failure cases, especially how the paper separates planning failures from evidence-integration failures. For a quick read, 20–30 minutes on setup, main results, and representative trajectories is enough. For deeper reading, the useful question is not whether LLMs fail, but what kind of inductive bias is missing: search policy, external memory, symbolic state abstraction, or training on information-gathering behavior itself.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看任务形式和错误轨迹分析即可；它把“agent 会不会学世界模型”拆成了干净可控的反例生成器。","en":"Read the task setup and failure-trajectory analysis; it gives a clean counterexample generator for claims about agents learning world models."},"metadata":{"institutions":["The Hebrew University of Jerusalem","New York University","Google Research"],"authors":["Reef Menaged","Gili Lior","Shauli Ravfogel","Roee Aharoni","Gabriel Stanovsky"],"notable_authors":["Shauli Ravfogel","Roee Aharoni","Gabriel Stanovsky"],"tags":["agent-evaluation","world-models","automata-learning","tool-use"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16576","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.16576","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.16821","title":"How Much Can We Trust LLM Search Agents?
