Retrieval-augmented generation avoids such parameter drift, yet often lacks the depth of parametric knowledge integration. In this paper, we propose ReGrad (Retrievable Gradients), a new paradigm that treats gradients as retrievable units of knowledge. ReGrad pre","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-14T10:31:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_15734.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.IR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决的是持续后训练中的“知识更新”和“权重漂移”冲突。CPT 可以把新知识写入参数，但反复更新同一组共享参数会累积漂移，带来遗忘和通用能力下降。RAG 可以避免改参数，但新知识只通过上下文注入，不一定能像参数知识一样被模型使用。ReGrad 试图在两者之间找一个折中：把梯度当作可检索的参数化知识单元，在需要时临时写入模型，用完即丢弃。","importance":"对预训练和持续学习研究的启发在于，它把“更新模型”从一次性修改全局权重，改成了检索式、局部化、可逆的参数适配。这个视角比普通 adapter/RAG 更接近训练动态本身：梯度不只是优化中间量，也可以被看作某个文档对参数空间的方向性约束。贡献主要是范式层面的，尤其适合思考 continual pretrain / post-train 的接口：新知识是否必须永久合并进基座参数，还是可以以可索引的参数增量形式存在。限制也比较明确：梯度存储、检索延迟、临时更新成本、梯度间干扰，以及安全回滚，都决定了它距离大规模在线服务还有工程门槛。","technical_approach":"ReGrad 分两阶段。离线阶段，对每个文档计算文档特定梯度，并存入带索引的 Gradient Bank。在线阶段，对用户 query 检索相关梯度，只把这部分梯度临时应用到模型权重上，再进行生成；生成结束后丢弃临时权重，恢复基座模型。这样避免把所有文档的更新累积到一个共享参数状态里，也避免无关知识对当前 query 产生参数干扰。\n\n关键设计不是简单缓存 next-token LM 梯度。原始语言建模梯度主要优化文档重构，容易学到表层 token 模式；下游知识使用需要回答问题、抽取隐含事实、对文档内容做推理。为此，ReGrad 引入 bi-level meta-learning：内层用文档诱导的梯度更新模型，外层用下游监督评价更新后的模型表现。这个目标会调整梯度的形成和应用方式，使临时适配更接近 query-driven 的知识使用，而不是复述文档。\n\n它可以单独使用，不把检索文本放入上下文，只通过参数临时适配注入知识；也可以和 RAG 结合，同时提供文本证据和可检索梯度。这个组合很重要，因为文本上下文负责显式证据，梯度适配负责改变模型的参数状态，两者不是同一种信号。","experiments":"给定正文显示实验覆盖通用场景和领域场景，包括法律与医学。主要结论有两点：第一，在 parameter-only 设置下，ReGrad 优于 continual post-training 和其他参数更新基线，说明文档梯度可以作为有效的可检索适配单元；第二，和 in-context RAG 结合后性能进一步提升，说明可检索梯度与文本检索提供互补信号。正文片段没有给出完整表格、参数规模、数据规模和具体提升数字，因此不宜补写精确数值。值得关注的消融应包括：原始 LM 梯度与 meta-learned 梯度的差异、检索 top-k 梯度数量、Gradient Bank 规模、只用梯度与梯度+文本的对比、对遗忘和通用能力保持的影响。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 CPT 和 RAG 取舍的定义，这部分给出了方法动机。然后重点看方法章节里的 Gradient Bank、在线临时权重适配、bi-level meta-learning 目标。实验部分优先看三类表格或图：ReGrad 与 的关系，尤其是梯度存储成本和服务时动态更新成本。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a concrete failure mode in continual post-training: newly acquired knowledge is written into the same shared weights, so repeated updates accumulate weight drift and can degrade general capabilities. RAG avoids this by keeping knowledge outside the model, but the knowledge is injected through context rather than through parameter-space adaptation. ReGrad proposes a middle ground: store document-induced gradients as retrievable knowledge units, apply only query-relevant gradients temporarily at inference time, and restore the base model after generation.","importance":"The main value is the reframing of gradients as reusable units of parametric memory. For continual pretraining and post-training, this changes the design space from permanent global weight updates to selective and reversible adaptation. It is especially relevant to researchers studying model updating, forgetting, and the pretrain–posttrain boundary. The idea is not just another RAG variant: it asks whether parametric knowledge can be externalized without losing the computational path of parameter adaptation. The practical risks are also clear: gradient-bank storage, retrieval quality, inference-time update overhead, gradient interference, and safe rollback all matter for scaling.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"The provided content reports experiments in both general and domain-specific settings, including law and medicine. Rollouts are sampled from the original prompt only, so the generation condition matches deployment. Expert traces are used only during optimization through two signals. The first is a trace-conditioned RL surrogate, where the expert trace acts as privileged context for shaping policy updates but is not exposed during rollout. The second is component-aware SFT, which supervises selected reasoning prefixes and complete tool calls rather than the entire trace. Separate annealing schedules reduce imitation pressure over training, giving early process guidance while avoiding long-term over-imitation. Prompt-only anchoring routes a subset of prompts to standard RL without trace losses, reducing dependence on privileged context. 惩罚效率低。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15–25 分钟。优先看 Background and Motivation，尤其是 Figure 1 的幻觉 token 稀疏性分析，这是方法成立的关键。然后看 BALTO 如何从 claim verification 映射到 token-level advantages，以及正负 advantage 如何归一和平衡。实验部分重点看 Acr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T23:30:14Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2606_15017.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是如何再给 一并计入。作者把问题重新表述为预算分配：同样的总 token，是拿去做在线技能/记忆机制，还是直接给 vanilla actor 更多 observe-act 轮次。这个问题对 agent 评测很关键，因为在线模块的收益依赖任务顺序、历史轨迹质量和模块调用频率，而这些都可能让看似更复杂的系统在真实预算约束下并不划算。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，但有两点启发很直接。第一，它提醒人们在评估“外部脚手架是否提升模型能力”时，必须把总推理成本算清楚；否则容易把额外 token 换来的收益误判成方法优势。这个判断对 test-time scaling、tool-augmented inference、memory-augmented decoding 乃至 post-train agent scaffold 都成立。第二，它指出单次 aggregate success rate 对在线 agent 很不稳，任务级方差和运行顺序会显著影响结论。这对任何把 test-time adaptation 当作能力来源的方法都有参考意义：如果收益主要来自额外预算和顺序偶然性，而不是更好的 credit assignment 或知识复用机制，那么方法价值需要重估。贡献类型更接近 evaluation protocol correction，而不是新算法。","technical_approach":"方法设计很朴素，但切中问题。作者选了三类已有在线增强方法：AWM（在线 workflow memory）、ASI（在线 skill induction）和 ReasoningBank（在线推理/经验库）。这三类方法都保留默认 actor horizon，最多 10 步，同时在每个任务或跨任务过程中额外消耗 token 去做工作流抽取、技能归纳、记忆检索、经验注入等操作。对照组 Vanilla-IB 不引入任何辅助模块，只把同等预算重新分配给更长的 actor interaction horizon。关键点不在于提出新 scaffold，而在于把 baseline 改成 budget-matched control：增强方法花在模块上的 token，vanilla baseline 则花在更多页面观察、状态推理和动作尝试上。\n\n实验设置强调在线性。任务按顺序处理，后续任务只能利用前面任务积累的模块知识，不能离线预先构建完整技能库。这一点很重要，因为在线模块的收益上限受任务排列和可复用模式密度约束，也限制了并行化。作者因此把问题限定在 deployment-like online setting，而不是离线蒸馏好技能后再评测的 setting。\n\n论文的核心技术贡献不是模块内部机制分析，而是评测口径的重构：\n1. 统一统计 total token usage，而不是只看 actor token；\n2. 用同预算 vanilla baseline 作为必要对照；\n3. 报告 multi-run variance，而不是依赖单次运行的 aggregate success rate。\n这三个点合起来，实际上是在挑战一类常见实验叙事：复杂 agent scaffold 的收益，可能只是预算不对齐下的表面优势。","experiments":"实验覆盖 WebArena 和 WorkArena-L1。WebArena 部分最完整，包含 3 个站点域：Shopping（187 个任务）、Reddit（106 个任务）、Admin（182 个任务），以及 3 个底座模型：Gemini 3 Flash、GPT-5.4-mini、Qwen 3.6-27B。比较对象是 AWM、ASI、ReasoningBank 和 Vanilla-IB。文中给出的总体结论很明确：在 WebArena 上，Vanilla-IB 在三种模型上的 aggregate success rate 都达到最高，且多数情况下总 token 还低于三种在线增强方法。也就是说，把预算直接给 actor 更多交互步，整体上比把预算分给在线技能/记忆模块更划算。\n\nWorkArena-L1 用 Qwen 3.6-27B 做同样比较，任务数 33，覆盖 ServiceNow 场景下的 dashboard、form、knowledge-base search、list filtering/sorting、menu navigation、service-catalog request 等企业知识工作流程。这里作者没有声称 Vanilla-IB 全面领先，而是说它与最优增强方法保持竞争力，说明“预算匹配后复杂模块未必占优”并不是 WebArena 特有现象。\n\n另一个重要实验观察是方差。作者指出单次运行的 aggregate success rate 会掩盖显著的 task-level variance；在线模块因为依赖历史任务积累，任务顺序和随机性会放大波动，所以单 run 比较不足以支持稳定结论。虽然给定内容里没有完整表格数字，但从正文结论看，方差分析是论文的重要补充证据，而不是附带现象。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不是学一个新 agent 算法，而是看作者如何把评测问题拆开。建议先看 Introduction，尤其是 Figure 1 附近关于 token allocation tradeoff 的论证，那里把问题讲得最清楚。然后看 Experimental Settings，确认四种 agent scaffold 的预算口径是否真正对齐，尤其要分清“默认 10-step actor + 辅助模块”与“无模块但更长 horizon”的差别。结果部分重点看两类内容：一是 WebArena aggregate result，二是 multi-run variance 分析。如果时间有限，优先读结论支撑 strongest 采样，哪些已有结论会改变。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a simple but under-reported question in online web agents: under a fixed inference budget, are memory / workflow / skill modules actually worth the tokens they consume? Prior agent papers often report success gains from auxiliary modules without fully accounting for the extra token cost of inducing skills, retrieving memories, injecting workflows, or running controller prompts. The authors recast the issue as a budget allocation problem: if the same total token budget were instead spent on additional observe-act steps by a plain actor, would the gains from online augmentation still hold? This matters because online modules are paid for on every task, depend on task order, and may trade away direct environment interaction for auxiliary processing.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research in two ways. First, it is a clean reminder that any claim about external scaffolding improving model capability must be normalized by total inference cost, not just actor-side cost. That lesson transfers to test-time scaling, tool-augmented reasoning, memory-augmented inference, and many post-training agent recipes. Second, the paper shows that single-run aggregate success can be misleading in online settings because task order and stochasticity materially affect outcomes. For researchers studying adaptation at inference time, the paper is useful as an evaluation correction: some apparent gains may come from extra budget and favorable sequencing rather than better reuse mechanisms.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"Read this as an evaluation paper, not as a new agent-method paper. For SFT or RL data for tool-using models, this suggests that correctness should be tied to an evidence chain from observation to action, not only to the final API call.","technical_approach":"ACCORD uses a two-layer mechanism. At inference time, it intercepts each write action before execution, identifies the contextual facts needed by that action, and reconstructs an action-ready grounded state from prior observations, live tool responses, returned data, and tool schemas. At the policy layer, it prompts the main agent to gather sufficient environmental information earlier in the trajectory. whether an agent can remember, but whether it can forget on command and do so correctly. It separates agent memory into a recall plane, which retrieves stored facts, and a control plane, which mutates them through operations such as supersede, release, and purge. The central question is where the LLM is placed relative to these two planes, and how that placement determines which forgetting failures the system can recover from. Existing memory systems and benchmarks mostly optimize retrieval quality, but they rarely test whether stale facts can be overwritten, temporary facts can expire, entity-specific memories can be deleted, or only the latest state remains reachable after a chain of updates.","importance":"Its value to foundation-model researchers is indirect but real.
